# Agents · R · part 1

1,027 entries, R-adversarial to README. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **R-adversarial**: Red-team / devil's advocate for specs, diffs, analyses, proposals, architecture, and ideas. Attacks assumptions, control effectiveness, vacuous guards, fleet impact, and partial-failure paths. On `/R-dev-review`: also apply an OWASP lens (secrets, injection, auth) — R-security-auditor is not spawned by default. Invoked by `/R-adversarial` (standalone on any design subject), `/R-spec` (Step 4 — Expert Review, always), and `/R-dev-review` (Phase 3 — Multi-Domain Review, always). Read-only: findings only, never fixes. &lt;example&gt; Context: Spec for a release gate about to ship user: "/R-spec --issu… ([Roxabi/roxabi-plugins](https://github.com/Roxabi/roxabi-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev-core/agents/R-adversarial.md) · ★9)
- **R-architect**: Use this agent for system design decisions, cross-cutting architecture, and technical planning across the monorepo. Two modes: **normal** (design + ADRs) and **axial** (read-only N×M / target-axis-trap review against the unique `axial: true` ADR). Axial create/supersede is `/R-adr --axial`, not this agent. &lt;example&gt; Context: New feature requires architectural decisions user: "Design the caching strategy for the API" assistant: "I'll use the R-architect agent to design the architecture." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: Spec proposes a new integration target; axial ADR exists user: "/R-spec --iss… ([Roxabi/roxabi-plugins](https://github.com/Roxabi/roxabi-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev-core/agents/R-architect.md) · ★9)

- **r-package-reviewer**: R package source reviewer. Use after writing or modifying package source (R/, tests/, DESCRIPTION, NAMESPACE), or as the review pass inside /r-package-check. - **r-package-reviewer**: Independent review of a devPTIpack diff for R-package conventions — NAMESPACE coherence, @export presence on exported fns, examples runnable with bundled data only, no debug artefacts (browser, profvis, library() inside R/), roxygen2 fields complete per .claude/rules/roxygen-documentation.md, R CMD check expected to stay clean. Use this agent before merging any PR on the redesign branch. Returns a structured findings list — does not modify code.
